TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合Hessian平均、sketch-and-project求解器和Nesterov加速的在线牛顿方法，在保持O(d²)复杂度（与一阶方法相当）的同时实现了二阶方法的鲁棒性，并提供了完整的统计推断框架，包括全局几乎必然收敛性、最后一个迭代点的渐近正态性以及全在线协方差估计器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线决策中，一阶方法虽计算高效但推断需要更新协方差矩阵（O(d²)复杂度）且对病态条件和噪声异质性敏感；二阶方法更鲁棒但求解牛顿系统需O(d³)复杂度。本文旨在填补这一空白，在二阶方法的鲁棒性与计算效率之间取得平衡。

Method: 提出了一种在线牛顿方法，核心创新包括：(1) Hessian平均技术；(2) 使用sketch-and-project求解器结合Nesterov加速来近似计算每一步的牛顿方向，从而将复杂度降至O(d²)；(3) 建立了完整的理论分析框架，包括随机数据和随机计算的双重不确定性量化。

Result: 在标准光滑性和矩条件下，建立了全局几乎必然收敛性；证明了最后一个迭代点的渐近正态性，其极限协方差由Lyapunov方程刻画；开发了全在线协方差估计器并具有非渐近收敛保证；将所得不确定性量化结果与精确牛顿方法和无Nesterov加速的sketched牛顿方法进行了对比。

Conclusion: 该方法在回归模型的广泛实验中证明了在线推断的优越性，在计算复杂度与统计推断质量之间实现了更优的权衡。

Abstract: Reliable decision-making with streaming data requires principled uncertainty quantification of online methods. While first-order methods enable efficient iterate updates, their inference procedures still require updating proper (covariance) matrices, incurring $O(d^2)$ time and memory complexity, and are sensitive to ill-conditioning and noise heterogeneity of the problem. This costly inference task offers an opportunity for more robust second-order methods, which are, however, bottlenecked by solving Newton systems with $O(d^3)$ complexity. In this paper, we address this gap by studying an online Newton method with Hessian averaging, where the Newton direction at each step is approximately computed using a sketch-and-project solver with Nesterov's acceleration, matching $O(d^2)$ complexity of first-order methods. For the proposed method, we quantify its uncertainty arising from both random data and randomized computation. Under standard smoothness and moment conditions, we establish global almost-sure convergence, prove asymptotic normality of the last iterate with a limiting covariance characterized by a Lyapunov equation, and develop a fully online covariance estimator with non-asymptotic convergence guarantees. We also connect the resulting uncertainty quantification to that of exact and sketched Newton methods without Nesterov's acceleration. Extensive experiments on regression models demonstrate the superiority of the proposed method for online inference.

</details>


### [77] [High-dimensional Semi-supervised Classification via the Fermat Distance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23573)
*Ruoxu Tan,Yiming Zang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出基于Fermat距离的加权k近邻分类器和MDS诱导分类器，用于高维半监督学习。理论证明加权k-NN是极小最优的，且未标注数据对Fermat距离估计的误差随合并样本量指数衰减。实验表明该方法优于主流图基半监督分类器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半监督学习中标注数据稀缺而未标注数据大量存在，在高维场景下这一问题尤为突出。现有方法难以同时利用流形假设和簇假设，缺乏理论保证。本文旨在通过Fermat距离这一密度敏感度量来编码簇假设，实现高效且可理论保证的半监督分类。

Method: 1) 基于Fermat距离构建加权k-NN分类器，利用密度敏感度量自然编码簇假设；2) 使用多维缩放(MDS)将高维流形数据投影到较大目标维度，使线性分类器可应用于复杂流形数据；3) 通过理论分析证明加权k-NN使用真实Fermat距离时的极小最优性，并量化未标注数据对估计误差的指数衰减贡献。

Result: 理论方面：推导出簇内期望过剩风险的尖锐下界，证明加权k-NN分类器是极小最优的；未标注数据带来的Fermat距离估计误差随合并样本量指数衰减，速率远快于文献中的相关结果。实验方面：在合成和真实数据集上的表现优于或媲美最先进的图基半监督分类器。

Conclusion: Fermat距离作为密度敏感度量能有效编码簇假设，其估计误差的指数衰减率充分体现了未标注数据的价值。所提方法在理论和实践层面均优于现有图基半监督方法，为高维半监督分类提供了新的有效范式。

Abstract: Semi-supervised classification, where unlabeled data are massive but labeled data are limited, often arises in machine learning applications. We address this challenge under high-dimensional data by leveraging the manifold and cluster assumptions. Based on the Fermat distance, a density-sensitive metric that naturally encodes the cluster assumption, we propose the weighted $k$-nearest neighbors (NN) classifier and multidimensional scaling (MDS)-induced classifiers. The use of MDS with a large target dimension allows the effective application of linear classifiers to complex manifold data. Theoretically, we derive a sharp lower bound for the expected excess risk within clusters and prove that the weighted $k$-NN classifier utilizing the true Fermat distance is minimax optimal. Furthermore, we explicitly quantify the utility of unlabeled data by showing that the error arising from estimating the Fermat distance decays exponentially with the pooled sample size. Such a rate is much faster than the related rates in the literature. Extensive experiments on synthetic and real datasets demonstrate competitive or superior performance of our approaches compared to state-of-the-art graph-based semi-supervised classifiers.

</details>


### [78] [Sliced-Regularized Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23944)
*Khai Nguyen*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的正则化最优传输（OT）方法——切片正则化最优传输（SROT），它使用平滑后的切片OT（SOT）计划作为参考正则化项，而非像熵正则化OT（EOT）那样使用独立耦合。SROT在相同正则化水平下比EOT更精确地逼近真实OT计划，并改进了参考SOT计划本身。文中给出了SROT的形式化定义、对偶形式、后验贝叶斯解释、Sinkhorn风格算法以及SROT散度的拓扑与计算性质分析，并通过合成数据、颜色迁移和梯度流实验验证了其优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典OT计算成本高，EOT虽可扩展但正则化向独立耦合会导致对真实OT计划的近似偏差较大。作者希望找到一个更优的正则化参考分布，使得在保持EOT计算优势的同时，获得更准确的OT近似。

Method: 将平滑后的SOT计划作为正则化参考，替代EOT中的独立耦合；推导SROT的对偶形式和后验贝叶斯解释；设计基于Sinkhorn算法的迭代求解方案，保持与EOT相同的计算复杂度和可扩展性；定义SROT散度并分析其拓扑性质。

Result: SROT在相同正则化强度下比EOT更逼近精确OT计划；SROT计划改进了参考SOT计划本身；合成数据和颜色迁移实验验证了SROT优于EOT和SOT；梯度流实验展现了SROT散度的优势。

Conclusion: SROT是一种有效的正则化OT新范式，兼具EOT的可扩展性和更优的近似精度，在OT计划逼近和散度应用上均优于现有方法。

Abstract: We propose a new regularized optimal transport (OT) formulation, termed sliced-regularized optimal transport (SROT). Unlike entropic OT (EOT), which regularizes the transport plan toward an independent coupling, SROT regularizes it toward a smoothened sliced OT (SOT) plan. To the best of our knowledge, SROT is the first approach to leverage a version of SOT plan as a reference to improve classical OT. We provide a formal definition of SROT, derive its dual formulation, and provide a post-Bayesian interpretation of SROT. We then develop a Sinkhorn-style algorithm for efficient computation, retaining the same scalability advantages as EOT. By incorporating a scalable SOT plan as a prior, SROT yields more accurate approximations of the exact OT plan than EOT under the same level of regularization. Moreover, the resulting transport plan improves upon the reference SOT plan itself. We further introduce the corresponding OT divergence induced by SROT, named SROT divergence, and analyze its topological and computational properties. Finally, we validate our approach through experiments on synthetic datasets and color transfer tasks, demonstrating that SROT is better than both EOT and SOT in approximating exact OT. Additional experiments on gradient flows further highlight the advantages of SROT divergence.

</details>

*Ludovico T. Giorgini*

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于条件得分（conditional score）的新型随机降阶模型校准方法，通过将漂移和扩散系数与有限时间转移密度的条件得分相关联，将模型拟合转化为关于平稳滞后对的线性最小二乘问题，无需轨迹微分、状态空间划分或重复积分候选模型，可处理高维、粗采样或不均匀采样数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有随机降阶模型校准方法（如短时轨迹增量、状态空间划分、重复模拟候选模型）在高维系统、粗时间采样或不均匀采样数据下不可靠或计算成本过高，亟需一种可扩展且鲁棒的校准方法。

Method: 推导出随机降阶模型系数与有限时间转移密度的条件得分（即关于初始状态的转移密度对数梯度）之间的新恒等式，将滞后相关函数的导数表达为涉及条件得分和未知系数的平稳期望，从而将漂移和扩散结构的约束转化为关于平稳滞后对的最小二乘拟合问题。

Result: 在解析可解和非平衡扩散数据集上验证，所推断的模型能保持不变统计量，并准确再现有限时间动力相关性。

Conclusion: 该方法为从数据中学习能够再现指定统计和动力学性质的随机降阶模型提供了可扩展的途径。

Abstract: Stochastic reduced-order models are widely used to represent the effective dynamics of complex systems, but estimating their drift and diffusion coefficients from data remains challenging. Standard approaches often rely on short-time trajectory increments, state-space partitioning, or repeated simulation of candidate models, which become unreliable or computationally expensive for high-dimensional systems, coarse temporal sampling, or unevenly sampled data. We introduce a data-driven calibration method based on a novel relationship between the coefficients of a stochastic reduced model and the conditional score of the finite-time transition density, defined as the gradient of the logarithm of the transition density with respect to the initial state. The resulting identity expresses derivatives of lagged correlation functions as stationary expectations over observed lagged pairs involving this conditional score and the unknown model coefficients. This formulation allows the drift and diffusion structure to be constrained directly from finite-lag statistics, without differentiating trajectories, partitioning state space, or repeatedly integrating candidate reduced models during calibration, yielding a least-squares fitting problem over stationary lagged pairs. We validate the approach on analytically tractable and data-driven nonequilibrium diffusions, demonstrating that the inferred models preserve the invariant statistics while accurately reproducing finite-lag dynamical correlations. The framework provides a scalable route for learning stochastic reduced-order models from data that reproduce prescribed statistical and dynamical properties.

</details>


### [80] [A Divergence-Based Method for Weighting and Averaging Model Predictions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24172)
*Olav Benjamin Vassend*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于最小散度框架的模型权重计算方法，用于平均来自统计和机器学习模型的概率预测。该方法通用性强，适用于频率学派、贝叶斯及其他拟合方法。实证表明，尤其在样本量较小时，该方法优于或等同于标准模型平均方法（如模型堆叠和基于AIC指数加权的模型平均）。理论分析解释了其小样本优势。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 基于最小散度（minimum divergence）框架推导新的模型权重计算公式，该方法不依赖于模型的拟合方法（频率学派、贝叶斯等均可适用），并从两个不同角度进行了理论动机解释。

Result: 实证结果显示，新方法在性能上优于或等同于标准模型平均方法，特别是在小样本条件下优势明显。理论分析揭示了小样本优势的内在原因。

Conclusion: 本研究提出的最小散度加权方法是一种通用、有效的概率预测模型平均方案，尤其适合小样本场景，为模型平均领域提供了新的理论视角和实用工具。

Abstract: This paper uses a minimum divergence framework to introduce a new way of calculating model weights that can be used to average probabilistic predictions from statistical and machine learning models. The method is general and can be applied regardless of whether the models under consideration are fit to data using frequentist, Bayesian, or some other fitting method. The proposed method is motivated in two different ways and is shown empirically to perform better than or on a par with standard model averaging methods, including model stacking and model averaging that relies on Akaike-style negative exponentiated model weighting, especially when the sample size is small. Our theoretical analysis explains why the method has a small-sample advantage.

</details>


### [81] [Identifiability and Stability of Generative Drifting with Companion-Elliptic Kernel Families](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24196)
*Hak Geun Lee*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文分析了生成性漂移框架中分布匹配的漂移场的可辨识性与稳定性。引入companion-elliptic核类（包含Laplace核），证明该类核下漂移场为零当且仅当两概率测度相等；通过反例说明场范数控制不足以保证弱收敛，但证明失效模式仅局限于一维射线，通过引入本征重叠标量的渐近下界可恢复弱收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在Deng等人提出的生成性漂移(Generative Drifting)框架中，漂移场用于分布匹配，但其可辨识性（漂移场能否唯一识别分布差异）和稳定性（场范数控制是否能保证分布弱收敛）尚未得到充分的理论分析。

Method: 1. 引入companion-elliptic核类，刻画了核κ与其companion函数η之间的二阶椭圆型耦合关系。\n2. 证明该类核（精确表征为高斯核和ν≥1/2的Matérn核）满足：漂移场为零 ⇔ 两Borel概率测度相等。\n3. 构造反例展示质量逃逸至无穷而场趋于零的序列。\n4. 证明失效模式仅限于一维射线{c p: 0≤c≤1}。\n5. 提出通过本征重叠标量(intrinsic overlap scalar)的渐近下界约束来恢复弱收敛。

Result: 1. 确定了companion-elliptic核类（Laplace核、高斯核、Matérn核(ν≥1/2)）具有分布匹配的可辨识性。\n2. 发现场范数控制不能保证弱收敛，存在质量逃逸的反例。\n3. 定位了失效模式的结构（仅出现在一维射线上）。\n4. 提供了通过本征重叠标量恢复弱收敛的理论方案。

Conclusion: 本文建立了生成性漂移框架中漂移场的理论基础，明确了其可辨识性的核条件，揭示了场范数稳定性的局限及其补救方案，对基于漂移场的分布匹配方法提供了重要的理论指导。

Abstract: This paper analyzes identifiability and stability for the drifting field underlying distributional matching in the Generative Drifting framework of Deng et al. First, we introduce the class of companion-elliptic kernels, which includes the Laplace kernel and is characterized by a second-order elliptic coupling between each kernel $κ$ in this class and its companion function $η$. For each kernel in this class and each pair of Borel probability measures, we prove that the drifting field vanishes if and only if the two probability measures are equal. We further show that this class consists precisely of Gaussian kernels and Matérn kernels with $ν\ge 1/2$. Second, by constructing counterexamples, we exhibit sequences for which mass escapes to infinity while the field tends to zero; in particular, control of the field norm alone does not guarantee weak convergence. Nevertheless, we prove that the only possible mode of failure is confined to the one-dimensional ray $\{c\,p:0\le c\le 1\}$. Consequently, weak convergence can be restored by imposing an asymptotic lower bound on the intrinsic overlap scalar, a linear observable defined by the kernel and the target measure.

</details>


### [82] [Extreme bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24545)
*Alexandra Carpentier,Michal Valko*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究在有限反馈下顺序分配资源以检测极端值的问题，提出ExtremeHunter算法并分析其极端遗憾，在合成和真实数据上验证效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在许多领域（医学、安全、生命科学）中，我们需要将有限资源分配给不同信源以检测极端值，而现有的bandit理论主要优化平均奖励的最大遗憾，不适用于检测最极端值的场景。

Method: 提出ExtremeHunter算法，用于在有限反馈下顺序分配资源，优化极端遗憾（即与选择最重尾信源的oracle策略相比的效率）。

Result: 在合成数据和真实世界实验上进行了实证评估。

Conclusion: ExtremeHunter算法能有效检测极端值，弥补了传统bandit方法在极端值检测问题上的不足。

Abstract: In many areas of medicine, security, and life sciences, we want to allocate limited resources to different sources in order to detect extreme values. In this paper, we study an efficient way to allocate these resources sequentially under limited feedback. While sequential design of experiments is well studied in bandit theory, the most commonly optimized property is the regret with respect to the maximum mean reward. However, in other problems such as network intrusion detection, we are interested in detecting the most extreme value output by the sources. Therefore, in our work we study extreme regret which measures the efficiency of an algorithm compared to the oracle policy selecting the source with the heaviest tail. We propose the ExtremeHunter algorithm, provide its analysis, and evaluate it empirically on synthetic and real-world experiments.

</details>


### [83] [Instrumental Variable Analysis Without Structural Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24660)
*Zikai Shen,Dimitri Meunier,Houssam Zenati,Arthur Gretton,Nathan Kallus,Aurélien Bibaut*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出对逆问题的最小二乘解进行去偏推断，避免假设精确解存在。该方法允许在结构模型不完全成立时仍保持推断有效性，并以工具变量为例展示了其应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的逆问题推断方法通常假设精确解存在，这一假设实质性强且并非无害。当该假设不成立时，基于此的统计推断可能失效。作者旨在开发一种不依赖精确解存在性的推断方法。

Method: 作者采用去偏推断（debiased inference）思路，对最小二乘解进行推断，而非假设存在精确解。该方法定义的推断目标量无论解是否存在都有定义，并在解存在时与通常的估计量一致。

Result: 该方法允许研究者用结构模型激励分析，但即使这些模型不完全成立，推断程序仍然有效。以工具变量为例，展示了该方法的实用性。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一种更稳健的逆问题推断框架，放宽了精确解存在的强假设，提高了推断的可靠性。

Abstract: We consider debiased inference on least-squares solutions to inverse problems as a way to avoid having to assume exact solutions exist. Such assumptions are substantive and not innocuous and their failure may well imperil inference when we impose them on the statistical model. Our approach instead allows us to conduct inference on a quantity that is defined regardless of solutions existing and coincides with the usual estimands when they do. For the case of instrumental variables, this means we can motivate the analysis with structural models but these do not need to hold exactly for the inferential procedure to remain valid.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [84] [The Spectral Lifecycle of Transformer Training: Transient Compression Waves, Persistent Spectral Gradients, and the Q/K--V Asymmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22778)
*Yi Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了Transformer预训练过程中权重矩阵奇异值谱的动态演化，发现了三种现象：瞬态压缩波、持久谱梯度和Q/K-V功能不对称，并据此提出了双时间尺度动力学模型和缩放定律，验证了谱结构对层重要性和剪枝策略的预测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多关注预训练结束时的权重谱特征，缺乏对训练过程中谱动态演化的系统理解，特别是不同层、不同参数矩阵之间的差异及其与训练机制的关联尚不明确。

Method: 对三个规模（30M-285M参数）的Transformer模型在25步间隔下对所有权重矩阵进行完整SVD分解，分析奇异值谱的动态变化；并在三个模型家族（自定义、GPT-2、Pythia；30M-1B参数；8-36层）上进行验证。

Result: 发现：（1）稳定秩压缩从浅层向深层传播，形成“瞬态压缩波”，后期深层压缩反超浅层；（2）幂律指数α形成永久性深度梯度，在更深模型中呈现非单调倒U型，峰值随深度增加向浅层移动；（3）值/输出投影矩阵压缩均匀，而查询/键投影矩阵承载完整的深度依赖动态；（4）谱引导剪枝在七个模型上比Last-N启发式剪枝提升1.1×-3.6×，最差与最佳差距达23.7×。

Conclusion: 秩与谱形状编码了训练过程中本质上不同的信息，谱结构具有因果关系而非仅仅是相关性，可用于预测层重要性和指导剪枝策略。

Abstract: We present the first systematic study of weight matrix singular value spectra \emph{during} transformer pretraining, tracking full SVD decompositions of every weight matrix at 25-step intervals across three model scales (30M--285M parameters). We discover three phenomena: \textbf{(1)~Transient Compression Waves:} stable rank compression propagates as a traveling wave from early to late layers, creating a dramatic gradient that peaks early then \emph{reverses} -- late layers eventually over-compress past early layers. \textbf{(2)~Persistent Spectral Gradients:} the power-law exponent~$α$ develops a permanent depth gradient forming a non-monotonic inverted-U in deeper models, with peaks shifting toward earlier layers as depth increases. \textbf{(3)~Q/K--V Functional Asymmetry:} value/output projections compress uniformly while query/key projections carry the full depth-dependent dynamics. The dissociation between transient compression and persistent spectral shape reveals that \emph{rank and spectral shape encode fundamentally different information about training}. We formalize this as a two-timescale dynamical model and derive scaling laws ($Δα\propto L^{0.26}$, $R^2{=}0.99$). We validate on nine models across three families (custom, GPT-2, Pythia; 30M--1B parameters; 8--36 layers), demonstrate that $α$ predicts layer importance ($ρ{=}0.69$--$0.84$, $p{<}0.02$), and show that spectral-guided pruning outperforms Last-N heuristics by $1.1{\times}$--$3.6{\times}$ across seven models in two families (GPT-2 124M--774M, Pythia 160M--1B), with worst-vs-best gaps up to $23.7{\times}$ confirming the causal role of spectral structure.

</details>


### [85] [KARL: Mitigating Hallucinations in LLMs via Knowledge-Boundary-Aware Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22779)
*Cheng Gao,Cheng Huang,Kangyang Luo,Ziqing Qiao,Shuzheng Si,Huimin Chen,Chaojun Xiao,Maosong Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: KARL框架通过知识边界感知奖励和两阶段强化学习训练策略，使LLM能够根据自身知识边界动态调整拒答行为，在抑制幻觉的同时保持高准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习方法使用静态奖励机制，忽略了模型自身的知识边界变化，导致模型过度谨慎或牺牲回答准确性，无法在准确性和幻觉之间取得良好平衡。

Method: 提出KARL框架，包含两个核心创新：(1) 知识边界感知奖励——通过组内响应统计进行在线知识边界估计，动态奖励正确答案或有引导的拒答；(2) 两阶段强化学习训练策略——第一阶段探索知识边界并绕过"拒答陷阱"，第二阶段将超出知识边界的错误回答转化为拒答而不牺牲准确性。

Result: 在多个基准测试上的实验表明，KARL实现了更优的准确率-幻觉权衡，在分布内和分布外场景中均能有效抑制幻觉同时保持高准确性。

Conclusion: KARL通过动态对齐模型的拒答行为与其不断演变的知识边界，有效解决了静态奖励机制导致的过度谨慎问题，为提升LLM可信度提供了新思路。

Abstract: Enabling large language models (LLMs) to appropriately abstain from answering questions beyond their knowledge is crucial for mitigating hallucinations. While existing reinforcement learning methods foster autonomous abstention, they often compromise answer accuracy because their static reward mechanisms, agnostic to models' knowledge boundaries, drive models toward excessive caution. In this work, we propose KARL, a novel framework that continuously aligns an LLM's abstention behavior with its evolving knowledge boundary. KARL introduces two core innovations: a Knowledge-Boundary-Aware Reward that performs online knowledge boundary estimation using within-group response statistics, dynamically rewarding correct answers or guided abstention; and a Two-Stage RL Training Strategy that first explores the knowledge boundary and bypasses the "abstention trap", and subsequently converts incorrect answers beyond the knowledge boundary into abstentions without sacrificing accuracy. Extensive experiments on multiple benchmarks demonstrate that KARL achieves a superior accuracy-hallucination trade-off, effectively suppressing hallucinations while maintaining high accuracy across both in-distribution and out-of-distribution scenarios.

</details>


### [86] [Stochastic KV Routing: Enabling Adaptive Depth-Wise Cache Sharing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22782)
*Anastasiia Filippova,David Grangier,Marco Cuturi,João Monteiro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出随机跨层注意力机制，通过在训练中让各层随机选择使用自身或前层的KV缓存，实现深度维度的缓存共享，在不损失信息的前提下显著减少Transformer语言模型推理时的KV缓存内存占用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer语言模型在自回归生成中需要缓存Key-Value来避免重复计算，但KV缓存的内存开销巨大，严重影响服务成本。虽然已有工作通过时间维度的压缩或淘汰来减少缓存，但深度维度尚未被有效利用。现有跨层缓存共享方法通常会导致吞吐量下降或首令牌延迟增加。

Method: 提出随机跨层注意力机制：训练过程中，每层以随机方式选择关注自身KV状态或前一层KV状态。这种随机化过程使模型对各种深度维度的缓存共享策略具有鲁棒性，同时保证部署时的灵活性。该方法可应用于预训练或微调阶段。

Result: 在多种模型家族上验证了该方案的有效性。在数据受限场景下的大模型中，该方法表现出类似正则化的效果，通常能保持甚至提升模型性能，同时显著降低缓存内存占用。

Conclusion: 深度维度的缓存共享是一种正交且稳健的优化方向，随机跨层注意力机制能够在不降低吞吐量或增加首令牌延迟的前提下，有效减少KV缓存内存需求。

Abstract: Serving transformer language models with high throughput requires caching Key-Values (KVs) to avoid redundant computation during autoregressive generation. The memory footprint of KV caching is significant and heavily impacts serving costs. This work proposes to lessen these memory requirements. While recent work has largely addressed KV cache reduction via compression and eviction along the temporal axis, we argue that the \emph{depth} dimension offers an orthogonal and robust avenue for optimization. Although prior research suggests that a full cache for every layer is redundant, implementing cross-layer cache sharing remains a practical challenge; existing methods typically suffer from reduced throughput or increased time-to-first-token. In this paper, we demonstrate that dropping a layer's cache offers efficient optimization without information loss. We propose a simple training approach: random cross-layer attention. During training, layers randomly choose to attend either to their own KV states or those of a preceding layer. This stochastic process adapts the model to be robust to various depth-wise cache sharing strategies, ensuring flexibility for unknown hardware constraints at deployment time. Our evaluations show that applying this scheme during pre-training or fine-tuning enables depth-wise cache sharing for various model families. Furthermore, for larger models in data-constrained settings, this approach is suggestive of a regularization-like effect, frequently preserving or improving performance while significantly reducing the cache's memory footprint.

</details>


### [87] [Parameter Efficiency Is Not Memory Efficiency: Rethinking Fine-Tuning for On-Device LLM Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22783)
*Irene Tenison,Stella Ahn,Miriam Kim,Ebtisam Alshehri,Lalana Kagal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LARS框架，通过约束激活子空间而非模型参数，将内存消耗与序列长度解耦，在GPU和CPU上分别比LoRA平均减少33.54%和51.95%内存占用，并能在树莓派等边缘设备上运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战"参数效率等同于内存效率"的普遍假设，指出LoRA和IA3等PEFT方法虽减少可训练参数，但中间张量随序列长度线性增长，导致设备端内存溢出。

Method: LARS通过约束训练过程中使用的激活子空间，直接针对内存消耗的主要来源，从根本上降低内存增长率，将内存消耗与序列长度解耦。

Result: 相比LoRA，在GPU上平均减少33.54%内存占用，CPU上减少51.95%；在推理、理解和长上下文数据集上保持竞争性精度和吞吐量；成功部署在树莓派和消费级CPU上。

Conclusion: LARS为资源受限硬件和边缘设备上的大模型个性化提供了可扩展路径，证明了激活子空间约束是一种比参数低秩约束更有效的内存优化策略。

Abstract: Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning (PEFT) has become the standard for adapting large language models (LLMs). In this work we challenge the wide-spread assumption that parameter efficiency equates memory efficiency and on-device adaptability. We show that this is not true - while methods like LoRA and IA3 significantly reduce trainable parameters, they remain bound by intermediate tensors that scale linearly with sequence length, often triggering out-of-memory errors on-device. In this work, we introduce LARS (Low-memory Activation-Rank Subspace), a novel adaptation framework that decouples memory consumption from sequence length. While prior PEFT methods apply low-rank constraints to model parameters, LARS instead constrains the activation subspace used during training, directly targeting the dominant source of memory consumption and fundamentally flattening the memory growth rate. LARS reduces the memory footprint by an average of 33.54% on GPUs and 51.95% on CPUs in comparison to LoRA across reasoning, understanding and long-context datasets using different models while maintaining competitive accuracy and throughput. Besides GPUs, we deploy on Raspberry Pi and consumer-grade CPUs to demonstrate that LARS provides a scalable path for sophisticated LLM personalization on resource-constrained hardware and edge devices.

</details>


### [88] [CoFi-PGMA: Counterfactual Policy Gradients under Filtered Feedback for Multi-Agent LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22785)
*Stela Tong,Elai Ben-Gal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对多智能体LLM系统中路由和协作两种机制导致的反馈过滤问题（选择门控反馈和共享奖励），提出CoFi-PGMA统一框架，通过基于边际贡献的反事实训练目标校正学习信号，并给出实用的训练算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM系统（路由机制和协作机制）中，每个智能体收到的学习信号被系统机制过滤：路由产生选择门控反馈（仅评估被选中的响应），协作产生共享奖励（掩盖了个体贡献）。这导致标准RLHF目标无法正确适用于多智能体场景。

Method: 提出CoFi-PGMA统一框架，推导基于边际贡献的反事实每个智能体训练目标。对路由系统，该目标对应选择门控反馈的离策略校正；对协作系统，则简化为留一法差分奖励进行信用分配。进一步分析softmax路由如何引入风险敏感激励，并提供集成反事实估计器、多轮感知奖励和策略优化的实用训练算法。

Result: 在真实世界推理数据集上验证了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: CoFi-PGMA为多智能体LLM系统在过滤反馈下的学习提供了统一的理论框架和实用算法，解决了路由和协作机制下的学习信号校正问题。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) deployments increasingly rely on multi-agent architectures in which multiple models either compete through routing mechanisms or collaborate to produce a final answer. In both settings, the learning signal received by each agent is filtered by the system mechanism. Routing produces selection-gated feedback where only the chosen response is evaluated, while collaboration produces shared rewards that obscure the individual contribution of each agent. As a result, standard RLHF objectives designed for a single deployed policy become misspecified. We introduce CoFi-PGMA (Counterfactual Policy Gradients under Filtered Feedback for Multi-Agent LLMs), a unified framework for learning under filtered feedback in multi-agent LLM systems. Our approach derives a counterfactual per-agent training objective based on marginal contribution, which corrects the learning signal under both routing and collaborative mechanisms. For routing systems, the objective corresponds to off-policy corrections for selection-gated feedback, while for collaborative systems it reduces to leave-one-out difference rewards for credit assignment. We further analyze how softmax routing induces risk-sensitive incentives and provide practical training algorithms that integrate counterfactual estimators, multiturn-aware rewards, and policy optimization methods, and demonstrate the approach on a real-world reasoning dataset.

</details>


### [89] [AutoCompress: Critical Layer Isolation for Efficient Transformer Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22786)
*Archit Thorat*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出AutoCompress方法，基于发现小Transformer中第0层承载了不成比例的关键信息（NTK重要性得分高出其他层60倍以上），设计了关键层隔离（CLI）架构：保持第0层全维度，中间层通过学习的瓶颈压缩，最终层恢复全维度。在GPT-2 Medium上实现2.47倍压缩比，困惑度204.5 vs 基线571.8。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小Transformer中存在层间重要性不均衡现象：第0层承载了远超其他层的任务关键信息（NTK重要性得分3.6 vs 其他层最大0.054，差距超60倍），现有压缩方法未能充分利用这一结构特性。

Method: 提出Critical Layer Isolation (CLI)架构：1）保护第0层全维度不压缩；2）中间层通过学习的瓶颈层进行压缩；3）最终层恢复全维度。整体构成AutoCompress方法。

Result: 在GPT-2 Medium（354.8M参数）上，CLI-GPT2仅用143.8M参数（59.5%参数减少，2.47倍压缩）在WikiText-103上达到204.5困惑度。消融实验表明，同等规模的均匀瓶颈基线仅达到571.8困惑度，验证了保护第0层的架构决策是性能提升的主因。

Conclusion: 保护关键层（尤其是第0层）而非简单均匀地缩小模型，是Transformer压缩的关键设计原则。代码和检查点已公开。

Abstract: We present AutoCompress, a transformer compression method motivated by an empirical finding: in small transformers, Layer 0 carries disproportionately high task-critical information, with an NTK-based importance score of 3.6 compared to a maximum of 0.054 for all other layers -- a gap of over 60x. Based on this finding, we propose Critical Layer Isolation (CLI), an architecture that protects Layer 0 at full dimensionality, compresses all intermediate layers through a learned bottleneck, and restores the full dimension at the final layer. Applied to GPT-2 Medium (354.8M parameters), CLI-GPT2 achieves 204.5 perplexity on WikiText-103 with only 143.8M parameters -- a 2.47x compression ratio and 59.5% parameter reduction. Crucially, an ablation study demonstrates that a uniform bottleneck baseline of comparable size achieves only 571.8 perplexity under identical training conditions, confirming that the architectural decision to protect Layer 0 -- rather than simply reducing model size -- is the primary driver of performance. Code and checkpoints are publicly available.

</details>


### [90] [Avionic Main Fuel Pump Simulation and Fault-Diagnosis Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22869)
*Felix Leonhard Janzen,Lukas Moddemann,Alexander Diedrich,Oliver Niggemann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对飞机主燃油泵系统因数据保护和部分可观测性导致训练数据不足的问题，提出了一种基于MATLAB/Simulink Simscape Fluids的高保真物理信息联合仿真，生成带健康与故障标注的时间序列数据，并利用无监督RNN-VAE进行异常检测、SOM-VAE进行运行模式离散化，验证了该基准的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多信息物理系统（尤其是飞机等关键应用）因数据保护问题和部分可观测性，缺乏训练异常检测与诊断算法的数据。

Method: 1. 构建基于MATLAB/Simulink Simscape Fluids的高保真物理信息联合仿真模型（飞机主燃油泵系统）；2. 生成带健康与故障模式标注的时间序列数据；3. 应用无监督RNN-VAE进行异常检测，SOM-VAE进行运行模式离散化。

Result: 成功生成了带标注的仿真时序数据，RNN-VAE和SOM-VAE能够分离健康与故障状态，验证了该基准数据集的可行性。

Conclusion: 所提出的高保真物理仿真与无监督学习方法可为关键系统中的异常检测提供可行的基准方案。

Abstract: In many cyber-physical systems, especially in critical applications such as aeroplanes, data to train anomaly detection and diagnosis algorithms is lacking due to data protection issues and partial observability. To combat this inherent lack of data, we introduce a high-fidelity, physics-informed co-simulation of a common aircraft main-fuel-pump system modelled in \textsc{MATLAB/Simulink Simscape Fluids}. We also describe its generated time-series data with health and fault mode annotations. To show feasibility of our benchmark, we apply an unsupervised Recurrent Variational Autoencoder (RNN-VAE) for anomaly detection and a SOM-VAE for operating mode discretization, trained to separate healthy and faulty conditions.

</details>


### [91] [Score-Repellent Monte Carlo: Toward Efficient Non-Markovian Sampler with Constant Memory in General State Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22948)
*Jie Hu,Lingyun Chen,Geeho Kim,Jinyoung Choi,Bohyung Han,Do Young Eun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Score-Repellent Monte Carlo (SRMC)框架，通过运行平均分数评估的指数倾斜来编码轨迹历史，实现通用且高效的记忆依赖采样，在连续和高维离散空间中均能降低方差并改善模式覆盖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有历史依赖采样方案仅在有限状态空间上可行，无法处理高维离散或连续状态空间中的长期方差缩减问题。

Method: 通过分数的运行平均来总结轨迹历史，构建指数倾斜的替代目标，形成无需归一化的通用MCMC封装器；利用随机逼近与受控马尔可夫噪声分析耦合演化。

Result: 建立了几乎必然收敛和联合中心极限定理；证明随着排斥强度α增加，渐近协方差以O(1/α)尺度减小；实验在连续目标和离散能量基模型上验证了方差降低和模式覆盖改善。

Conclusion: SRMC将有限状态空间的近零方差效应推广到一般状态空间，仅需常数级O(d)内存和适度的每步开销。

Abstract: History-dependent sampling can reduce long-run Monte Carlo variance by discouraging redundant revisits, but existing schemes typically encode history through empirical measure on finite state spaces, which is infeasible in high-dimensional discrete configuration spaces or ill-posed in continuous domains. We propose Score-Repellent Monte Carlo (SRMC) framework that summarizes trajectory history by a running average of score evaluations in $R^d$, where $d$ is the dimension of the score and state representation. This history is converted into a surrogate target through an exponential score tilt, indexed with $α$ that represents the strength of repellence in controlling the magnitude of the history-based repulsion. The surrogate family is normalization-free in the standard MCMC sense, yielding a generic wrapper: at each iteration, any base kernel targeting $π$ can instead be run on the current surrogate $π_{θ_n}$ while the history is updated online. We analyze the coupled evolution of the history recursion and Monte Carlo estimators using stochastic approximation with controlled Markovian noise, establishing almost sure convergence and a joint central limit theorem. We further identify regimes in which the asymptotic covariance decreases as $α$ increases, with scaling $O(1/α)$, extending the near-zero-variance effect of finite-state history-dependent samplers to general state spaces with constant memory. Experiments on continuous targets and discrete energy-based models demonstrate improved estimator variance and mode coverage, while retaining $O(d)$ memory usage and modest per-iteration overhead.

</details>


### [92] [When Policies Cannot Be Retrained: A Unified Closed-Form View of Post-Training Steering in Offline Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22873)
*Elias Hossain,Mohammad Jahid Ibna Basher,Ivan Garibay,Ozlem Garibay,Niloofar Yousefi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了离线强化学习中冻结策略在部署时的自适应问题，提出使用专家乘积（PoE）与目标条件先验结合的组合方法。核心发现是该方法表现为**优雅退化**而非普适性提升：在退化或随机先验下，精度加权组合仍锚定于冻结策略，而加性先验和仅先验方法则崩溃。作者推导了对角高斯策略下PoE与KL正则化之间的闭式等价关系。实验表明，在D4RL环境中存在4/5/3的HELP/FROZEN/HURT三分结果，且中等专家策略在所有组合规则下均表现不佳，最终将PoE和KL正则化定位为**以演员锚定的安全机制**。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际部署中离线强化学习的训练目标可能事后改变，但由于数据、成本或治理约束，已训练的演员无法重新训练，因此需要一种无需重新训练即可在部署时调整冻结策略的方法。

Method: 采用专家乘积（PoE）与目标条件先验组合的方式对冻结离线演员进行部署时自适应。推导了对角高斯演员与先验下PoE（系数α）与KL正则化（系数β=α/(1-α)）之间的闭式等价关系，并提出KL预算选择器用于恢复近最优操作点。

Result: 在四个D2RL环境（3900个MuJoCo片段）中观察到4/5/3的HELP/FROZEN/HURT三分结果。在六个更难的cell和两个AntMaze诊断中，中等专家策略在所有9个cell和所有α下均表现为HURT；AntMaze中行为克隆的冻结演员在所有组合规则下成功率均为零。

Conclusion: PoE和KL正则化自适应应被视为一种以演员锚定的安全机制，用于部署时的策略引导，而非通用的性能提升方法。其核心价值在于避免灾难性退化而非保证提升。

Abstract: Offline reinforcement learning (RL) can learn effective policies from fixed datasets, but deployment objectives may change after training, and in many applications the trained actor cannot be retrained because of data, cost, or governance constraints. We study deployment-time adaptation for frozen offline actors using Product-of-Experts (PoE) composition with a goal-conditioned prior. Our main practical finding is graceful degradation rather than universal performance gain: under degraded or random priors, precision-weighted composition remains anchored to the frozen actor, while additive and prior-only adaptation collapse, and a KL-budget selector often recovers a near-oracle operating point. We also make explicit a closed-form identity in the frozen-actor setting: for diagonal-Gaussian actors and priors, PoE with coefficient alpha yields the same deterministic policy as KL-regularized adaptation with beta = alpha / (1 - alpha), with posterior covariances differing only by a global scalar factor. Empirically, across four D4RL environments (3,900 MuJoCo episodes), we observe a 4/5/3 HELP/FROZEN/HURT split. Extending the analysis to six harder cells and two AntMaze diagnostics reveals an actor-competence ceiling: medium-expert remains HURT in all 9 cells at every tested alpha, while AntMaze with a behavior-cloned frozen actor yields zero success for all composition rules. Overall, PoE and KL-regularized adaptation are best viewed as a single actor-anchored safety mechanism for deployment-time steering.

</details>


### [93] [Shape of Memory: a Geometric Analysis of Machine Unlearning in Second-Order Optimizers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23046)
*Kennon Stewart*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出当前机器遗忘定义对二阶优化器不充分，比较了一阶和二阶学习者在数据删除任务中的表现。发现二阶优化器在优化器状态中表现出显著波动，表明存在一阶分析无法检测到的残留信息。只有当几何信息（或记忆）被擦除时，才能恢复稳定性和信息丢失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机器遗忘的定义对于二阶优化器而言不够明确，需要更精确地理解二阶优化器在数据删除场景下的行为特性。

Method: 通过比较一阶和二阶学习者在数据删除任务中的表现，利用不同程度的特征分解来模拟损失模型记忆，并分析优化器状态的变化。

Result: 一阶和二阶方法在性能和梯度上都能与理想反事实对齐，但二阶优化器状态表现出显著波动，表明存在一阶分析无法检测的残留信息。仅在受控状态扰动下擦除几何信息（记忆）后，才能恢复稳定性并消除信息残留。

Conclusion: 现有的机器遗忘定义对二阶优化器不够充分；二阶优化器中存在一阶分析无法捕捉的残留信息；需要通过控制状态扰动擦除几何信息才能实现真正的遗忘。

Abstract: We argue that current definitions of machine unlearning are underspecified for second-order optimizers. We compare first-order and second-order learners for their ability to handle the data deletion task with varying degrees of eigendecomposition to mimic the loss model memory. While both first and second-order methods realign with the ideal counterfactul in terms of performance and gradient, the second-order optimizer shows significant volatility in the optimizer state. This indicates residual information, supposedly deleted, that isn't detectable by first-order analysis. Various eigendecay treatments show that stability and information loss is regained only under controlled state pertubation where geometric information (or memory) is erased.

</details>


### [94] [MTServe: Efficient Serving for Generative Recommendation Models with Hierarchical Caches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22881)
*Xin Wang,Chi Ma,Shaobin Chen,Pu Wang,Menglei Zhou,Junyi Qiu,Qiaorui Chen,Jiayu Sun,Shijie Liu,Zehuan Wang,Lei Yu,Chuan Liu,Fei Jiang,Wei Lin,Hao Wang,Jiawei Jiang,Xiao Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MTServe通过引入层次化缓存管理系统，利用主机RAM作为可扩展备份存储来虚拟化GPU内存，解决生成式推荐系统中长用户历史重复编码导致的推理成本过高和KV缓存存储爆炸问题，在公共和生产数据集上实现了高达3.1倍的加速，命中率超过98.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式推荐（GR）具有优越的建模能力，但由于长用户历史的重复编码导致推理成本过高。跨请求KV缓存复用虽提供了显著的优化机会，但单个用户状态的庞大规模导致的存储爆炸远超物理GPU内存限制。

Method: 提出MTServe层次化缓存管理系统，通过主机RAM作为可扩展备份存储实现GPU内存虚拟化；引入混合存储布局、异步数据传输流水线和局部性驱动的替换策略等一系列系统级优化，以弥合不同层级之间的I/O差距。

Result: 在公共和生产数据集上，MTServe实现了高达3.1倍的推理加速，同时保持接近完美的缓存命中率（>98.5%）。

Conclusion: MTServe通过层次化缓存管理和系统级优化，有效解决了生成式推荐中长用户历史导致的推理成本和存储瓶颈问题，在不牺牲命中率的前提下实现了显著加速。

Abstract: Generative recommendation (GR) offers superior modeling capabilities but suffers from prohibitive inference costs due to the repeated encoding of long user histories. While cross-request Key-Value (KV) cache reuse presents a significant optimization opportunity, the massive scale of individual user states creates a storage explosion that far exceeds physical GPU limits. We propose MTServe, a hierarchical cache management system that virtualizes GPU memory by leveraging host RAM as a scalable backup store. To bridge the I/O gap between tiers, MTServe introduces a suite of system-level optimizations, including a hybrid storage layout, an asynchronous data transfer pipeline, and a locality-driven replacement policy. On both public and production datasets, MTServe delivers up to 3.1* speedup while maintaining near-perfect hit ratios (>98.5%).

</details>


### [95] [CODA: Coordination via On-Policy Diffusion for Multi-Agent Offline Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23308)
*Marcel Hedman,Kale-ab Abebe Tessera,Juan Claude Formanek,Anya Sims,Riccardo Zamboni,Trevor McInroe,John Torr,Elliot Fosong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CODA提出了一种基于扩散模型的多智能体轨迹生成方法，通过生成与当前联合策略一致的合成经验数据，解决了离线多智能体强化学习中因策略无法协同适应而导致的协调失败问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线多智能体强化学习面临协调失败问题：智能体在固定离线数据集上训练时，策略变化后无法相互协同适应，导致收敛到次优的联合行为。现有的基于扩散模型的数据增强方法生成的是静态增强数据集，不会随训练过程中联合策略的变化而演化，不足以促进多智能体协调。

Method: CODA是一个基于扩散的多智能体轨迹生成器，它通过条件采样生成与当前联合策略一致的合成经验数据，从而模拟在线策略学习过程，为智能体提供协同适应机制。该方法与算法无关，可作为数据增强模块集成到无模型和基于模型的离线强化学习管道中。

Result: CODA能够解决连续多项式博弈中的典型协调病理问题，并在更复杂的MaMuJoCo连续控制基准上取得了强劲的结果。

Conclusion: CODA通过更接近在线策略学习的方式，在离线设置下向协调行为迈出了有意义的一步，有效解决了离线多智能体强化学习中的协调失败问题。

Abstract: Offline multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) enables policy learning from fixed datasets, but is prone to coordination failure: agents trained on static, off-policy data converge to suboptimal joint behaviours because they cannot co-adapt as their policies change. We introduce CODA (Coordination via On-Policy Diffusion for Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning), a diffusion-based multi-agent trajectory generator for data augmentation that samples conditioned on the current joint policy, producing synthetic experience which reflects the evolving behaviours of the agents, thereby providing a mechanism for co-adaptation. We find that previous diffusion-based augmentation approaches are insufficient for fostering multi-agent coordination because they produce static augmented datasets that do not evolve as the current joint policy changes during training; CODA resolves this by more closely simulating on-policy learning and is a meaningful step toward coordinated behaviours in the offline setting. CODA is algorithm-agnostic and can be layered onto both model-free and model-based offline reinforcement learning pipelines as an augmentation module. Empirically, CODA not only resolves canonical coordination pathologies in continuous polynomial games but also delivers strong results on the more complex MaMuJoCo continuous-control benchmarks.

</details>


### [96] [Quantifying and Mitigating Self-Preference Bias of LLM Judges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22891)
*Jinming Yang,Chuxian Qiu,Zhenyu Deng,Xinshan Jiao,Tao Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种自动化框架用于量化和减轻LLM作为评估者时的自我偏好偏差（SPB），通过构建质量相等的回答对来分离评估能力与偏差倾向，并基于认知负荷分解的多维评估策略将SPB平均降低31.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM评估系统中，自我偏好偏差（SPB）严重影响了评估的可扩展性和可信度，而现有测量方法依赖昂贵的人工标注，且无法区分生成能力与评估立场，难以大规模部署。

Method: 构建质量差异可忽略的等质量回答对，实现无需人工金标准的偏差倾向与判别能力的统计分离；提出基于认知负荷分解的结构化多维评估策略以缓解偏差。

Result: 对20个主流LLM的实证分析表明，高级能力与低SPB往往不相关甚至负相关；所提多维评估策略平均降低SPB 31.5%。

Conclusion: 该自动化框架有效解决了SPB量化和缓解问题，为大规模自动化评估系统的可信部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: LLM-as-a-Judge has become a dominant approach in automated evaluation systems, playing critical roles in model alignment, leaderboard construction, quality control, and so on. However, the scalability and trustworthiness of this approach can be substantially distorted by Self-Preference Bias (SPB), which is a directional evaluative deviation in which LLMs systematically favor or disfavor their own generated outputs during evaluation. Existing measurements rely on costly human annotations and conflate generative capability with evaluative stance, and thus are impractical for large-scale deployment in real-world systems. To address this issue, we introduce a fully automated framework to quantifying and mitigating SPB, which constructs equal-quality pairs of responses with negligible quality differences, enabling statistical disentanglement of discriminability from bias propensity without human gold standards. Empirical analysis across 20 mainstream LLMs reveals that advanced capabilities are often uncorrelated, or even negatively correlated, with low SPB. To mitigate this bias, we propose a structured multi-dimensional evaluation strategy grounded in cognitive load decomposition, which reduces SPB by 31.5\% on average.

</details>


### [97] [StackFeat RL: Reinforcement Learning over Iterative Dual Criterion Feature Selection for Stable Biomarker Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22892)
*A. Yermekov,D. A. Herrera-Martí*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: StackFeat-RL 是一种通过 REINFORCE 策略梯度优化迭代双准则特征选择算法超参数的元学习框架，在 COVID-19 miRNA 和阿尔茨海默病分类任务中，以 3-4 倍更少的特征实现了最高预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维基因组数据（d ≫ n）中的特征选择需要同时满足准确性、稀疏性和稳定性，但现有方法要么需要手动设定阈值（如 mRMR、稳定性选择），要么在数据扰动下产生不稳定选择（如 Lasso、Boruta），要么完全忽略生物学结构。

Method: 提出 StackFeat-RL 元学习框架，使用 REINFORCE 策略梯度优化迭代双准则特征选择算法的超参数。双准则同时要求系数一致性和选择频率，避免单准则方法遗漏的两种失效模式；迭代累积通过大数定律提供收敛保证。

Result: 在 COVID-19 miRNA 数据（GSE240888，332 特征）和三个阿尔茨海默病分类任务（GSE84422，13237 基因；Normal vs. Possible、Probable、Definite AD）上，StackFeat-RL 相比 ElasticNet、Boruta、mRMR 和稳定性选择等方法，达到了最高预测精度，且所需特征数减少 3-4 倍。

Conclusion: StackFeat-RL 在高维基因组特征选择中能同时实现高精度、高稀疏性和高稳定性，是一种有效的生物标志物发现工具。

Abstract: Feature selection in high-dimensional genomic data ($d \gg n$) demands methods that are simultaneously accurate, sparse, and stable. Existing approaches either require manual threshold specification (mRMR, stability selection), produce unstable selections under data perturbation (Lasso, Boruta), or ignore biological structure entirely. We introduce StackFeat-RL, a meta-learning framework that optimises the hyperparameters of an iterative dual-criterion feature selection algorithm via REINFORCE policy gradients. The dual criterion, requiring both coefficient consistency and selection frequency, guards against two failure modes missed by single-criterion methods, while iterative accumulation provides convergence guarantees via the law of large numbers.
  On COVID-19 miRNA data (GSE240888, 332 features) and three Alzheimer's disease classification tasks (GSE84422, 13237 genes; Normal vs.\ Possible, Probable, and Definite AD), StackFeat-RL achieves the highest predictive accuracy among all evaluated methods, including ElasticNet, Boruta, mRMR, and stability selection, while requiring 3--4$\times$ fewer features.
  Keywords: feature selection, reinforcement learning, REINFORCE, elastic net, biomarker discovery, Alzheimer's disease, dual-criterion selection, protein interaction networks

</details>


### [98] [Rank, Head-Channel Non-Identifiability, and Symmetry Breaking: A Precise Analysis of Representational Collapse in Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23681)
*Giansalvo Cirrincione*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出Dong等人关于Transformer无残差连接和MLP时会出现秩坍缩的结论虽正确但不完整。主要发现：(1) 层归一化精确保持仿射秩；(2) 残差连接在测度论意义上可阻止秩坍缩，MLP的不可替代功能是生成超出原始词嵌入线性张成的特征方向；(3) 识别出与秩坍缩不同的"头-通道不可识别性"现象。提出位置门控输出投影(PG-OP)作为部分补救方案，并将四种坍缩现象统一在对称性破缺框架下。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 完善对Transformer架构中秩坍缩现象的理解，澄清层归一化、残差连接和MLP各自的实际作用，并识别被忽视的新问题。

Method: 理论分析结合实验验证。对层归一化进行仿射秩保持性分析；从测度论角度证明残差连接阻止秩坍缩；分析MLP生成新特征方向的能力；通过参数计数论证头-通道不可识别性；提出位置门控输出投影(PG-OP)作为部分补救方案。

Result: (1) 层归一化是"仿射秩中性"的；(2) 残差连接在测度论意义上足以阻止秩坍缩，MLP的真正作用是产生新特征方向；(3) 多头注意力存在头-通道不可识别性，MLP无法解决；(4) PG-OP可在低于1.6%参数开销下部分缓解该问题；(5) 四种坍缩现象可统一在对称性破缺框架下。

Conclusion: 对Transformer各组件的作用有了更精确的理解，识别了新的退化现象，并提出了统一的理论框架和部分解决方案。

Abstract: A widely cited result by Dong et al. (2021) showed that Transformers built from self-attention alone, without skip connections or feed-forward layers, suffer from rapid rank collapse: all token representations converge to a single direction. The proposed remedy was the MLP. We show that this picture, while correct in the regime studied by Dong, is incomplete in ways that matter for architectural understanding.
  Three results are established. First, layer normalisation is precisely affine-rank-neutral: it preserves the affine rank of the token representation set exactly. The widespread claim that LN "plays no role" is imprecise; the correct statement is sharper. Second, residual connections generically obstruct rank collapse in real Transformers such as BERT-base, in a measure-theoretic sense, without contribution from the MLP. The MLP's irreplaceable function is different: generating feature directions outside the linear span of the original token embeddings, which no stack of attention layers can produce. Third, a phenomenon distinct from rank collapse is identified: head-channel non-identifiability. After multi-head attention sums per-head outputs through the output projection, individual contributions cannot be canonically attributed to a specific head; n(H-1)d_k degrees of freedom per layer remain ambiguous when recovering a single head from the mixed signal. The MLP cannot remedy this because it acts on the post-summation signal.
  A constructive partial remedy is proposed: a position-gated output projection (PG-OP) at parameter overhead below 1.6% of the standard output projection. The four collapse phenomena identified in the literature -- rank collapse in depth, in width, head-channel non-identifiability, and entropy collapse -- are unified under a symmetry-breaking framework, each corresponding to a distinct symmetry of the Transformer's forward pass.

</details>


### [99] [A General Representation-Based Approach to Multi-Source Domain Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23790)
*Ignavier Ng,Yan Li,Zijian Li,Yujia Zheng,Guangyi Chen,Kun Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于马尔可夫毯分解的无监督域适应通用框架，将表征中的马尔可夫毯划分为标签的父节点、子节点和配偶节点，并建立了可辨识性保证，进而开发了实用的非参数方法处理多种分布偏移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无监督域适应方法在处理高维观测时，常依赖独立潜变量或不变标签分布等限制性假设来建立目标域联合分布的可辨识性，这限制了其在现实场景中的适用性。本文旨在回答"应该学习和迁移什么样的表征"这一核心问题，提出无需强假设的通用框架。

Method: 首先证明基于所有预测信息（即马尔可夫毯）学习表征在一般设置下是欠定的；进而提出将马尔可夫毯表征划分为标签的父节点、子节点和配偶节点，并证明该划分可实现域适应的可辨识性；基于理论分析，开发了非参数的实用方法处理各类分布偏移。

Result: 建立了基于马尔可夫毯分解的域适应可辨识性理论保证；提出了能够处理不同类型分布偏移的非参数域适应方法。

Conclusion: 通过将表征中的马尔可夫毯分解为标签的父节点、子节点和配偶节点，可以在一般设置下实现可辨识的域适应，突破了现有方法对独立潜变量或不变标签分布等限制性假设的依赖。

Abstract: A central problem in unsupervised domain adaptation is determining what to transfer from labeled source domains to an unlabeled target domain. To handle high-dimensional observations (e.g., images), a line of approaches use deep learning to learn latent representations of the observations, which facilitate knowledge transfer in the latent space. However, existing approaches often rely on restrictive assumptions to establish identifiability of the joint distribution in the target domain, such as independent latent variables or invariant label distributions, limiting their real-world applicability. In this work, we propose a general domain adaptation framework that learns compact latent representations to capture distribution shifts relative to the prediction task and address the fundamental question of what representations should be learned and transferred. Notably, we first demonstrate that learning representations based on all the predictive information, i.e., the label's Markov blanket in terms of the learned representations, is often underspecified in general settings. Instead, we show that, interestingly, general domain adaptation can be achieved by partitioning the representations of Markov blanket into those of the label's parents, children, and spouses. Moreover, its identifiability guarantee can be established. Building on these theoretical insights, we develop a practical, nonparametric approach for domain adaptation in a general setting, which can handle different types of distribution shifts.

</details>


### [100] [Accelerating Frequency Domain Diffusion Models with Error-Feedback Event-Driven Caching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22901)
*Dong Liu,Haisheng Wang,Yanxuan Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出E²-CRF方法，利用频域扩散模型的谱定位和镜像对称特性，通过闭环误差反馈系统自适应缓存Transformer KV特征，基于事件驱动的残差动态触发重计算，在保持样本质量的同时实现约2.2倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在时间序列生成中效果显著，但推理速度慢限制了实际部署，因此需要加速频域扩散模型的推理过程。

Method: 利用频域结构特性（谱定位和镜像对称），采用闭环误差反馈系统自适应缓存扩散步骤间的Transformer KV特征，通过事件驱动的残差动态而非固定调度触发重计算，选择性重计算高能量或快速变化的token，同时复用稳定高频分量的缓存特征。

Result: 在5个数据集上实现约2.2倍加速，同时保持样本质量；在标准正则性假设下给出了充分条件误差和复杂度界限。

Conclusion: 缓存策略自然契合扩散过程从结构到细节的渐进特性，有效加速频域扩散模型推理。

Abstract: Diffusion models achieve remarkable success in time series generation. However, slow inference limits their practical deployment. We propose E$^2$-CRF (Error-Feedback Event-Driven Cumulative Residual Feature caching) to accelerate frequency domain diffusion models. Our method exploits two structural properties: (1) spectral localization, where signal energy concentrates in low frequencies, and (2) mirror symmetry, which halves the effective frequency dimension. E$^2$-CRF uses a closed-loop error-feedback system that adaptively caches transformer KV features across diffusion steps. We trigger recomputation using event-driven residual dynamics instead of fixed schedules. Our method selectively recomputes high-energy or rapidly-changing tokens while reusing cached features for stable high-frequency components. E$^2$-CRF achieves ~2.2 speedup while maintaining sample quality. We demonstrate effectiveness on 5 datasets. Our caching strategy naturally aligns with the diffusion process's structure-to-detail progression. We include sufficient-condition error and complexity bounds under standard regularity assumptions (Appendix), alongside empirical validation. Our code is available at https://github.com/NoakLiu/FastFourierDiffusion and is also integrated in https://github.com/NoakLiu/FastCache-xDiT.

</details>


### [101] [Causal Representation Learning from General Environments under Nonparametric Mixing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23800)
*Ignavier Ng,Shaoan Xie,Xinshuai Dong,Peter Spirtes,Kun Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出在通用环境（general environments）下，通过利用因果机制中三阶导数以内的充分变化条件，可在非参数混合函数和非线性潜因果模型（如加性噪声或异方差噪声模型）中完全恢复潜在有向无环图（DAG）并识别潜变量（仅存在微小不确定性），这是首个在非参数混合下从通用环境完全恢复潜在DAG的工作，其假设比现有方法更宽松。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果表示学习依赖多种特定环境假设（如单节点干预、耦合干预、硬干预）或参数约束（如线性混合函数），这些假设在实际问题中常被违反，因此需要一种适用于更广泛环境类别的通用框架。

Method: 形式化了一套适用于通用环境的因果表示学习需求，并证明通过适当利用因果机制在直至三阶导数上的充分变化条件，可在非参数混合函数和非线性潜因果模型（如加性高斯噪声模型或异方差噪声模型）下实现潜在DAG的完全恢复和潜变量的识别。

Result: 这是首个在非参数混合假设下，从通用环境中完全恢复潜在DAG的理论结果。该成果匹配或优于许多现有工作，但所需的环境变化假设更为宽松。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法在更少的限制性假设下实现了因果表示学习的核心目标，为实际应用中更灵活的因果发现提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Causal representation learning aims to recover the latent causal variables and their causal relations, typically represented by directed acyclic graphs (DAGs), from low-level observations such as image pixels. A prevailing line of research exploits multiple environments, which assume how data distributions change, including single-node interventions, coupled interventions, or hard interventions, or parametric constraints on the mixing function or the latent causal model, such as linearity. Despite the novelty and elegance of the results, they are often violated in real problems. Accordingly, we formalize a set of desiderata for causal representation learning that applies to a broader class of environments, referred to as general environments. Interestingly, we show that one can fully recover the latent DAG and identify the latent variables up to minor indeterminacies under a nonparametric mixing function and nonlinear latent causal models, such as additive (Gaussian) noise models or heteroscedastic noise models, by properly leveraging sufficient change conditions on the causal mechanisms up to third-order derivatives. These represent, to our knowledge, the first results to fully recover the latent DAG from general environments under nonparametric mixing. Notably, our results match or improve upon many existing works, but require less restrictive assumptions about changing environments.

</details>


### [102] [Inverting Foundation Models of Brain Function with Simulation-Based Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23865)
*Niels Bracher,Xavier Intes,Stefan T. Radev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了是否可以利用脑活动基础模型反向恢复刺激参数。通过将脑模拟器TRIBEv2与LLM配对，使用仿真推演学习从脑图到刺激潜在参数的映射，验证了神经编码质量，并为脑基础模型的解码和逆向设计提供了初步方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索脑活动基础模型能否逆向使用——即可否从合成的脑活动中恢复刺激或其属性，从而验证神经编码质量并实现受控刺激生成。

Method: 使用脑模拟器TRIBEv2与LLM配对，LLM根据语言参数（效价、唤醒度、优势度）生成新闻标题；然后利用仿真推演（simulation-based inference）学习从脑图到潜在刺激参数的概率映射。

Result: 实验表明，刺激参数可以从预测的脑图中恢复，验证了神经编码的质量；同时LLM可作为受控刺激生成器用于模拟实验。

Conclusion: 该研究为脑基础模型的解码和逆向设计提供了初步方法，证明了在模拟环境中从脑活动反向恢复刺激参数的可行性。

Abstract: Foundation models of brain activity promise a new frontier for in silico neuroscience by emulating neural responses to complex stimuli across tasks and modalities. A natural next step is to ask whether these models can also be used in reverse. Can we recover a stimulus or its properties from synthetic brain activity? We study this question in a proof-of-concept setting using TRIBEv2. We pair the brain emulator with large language models (LLMs) that generate news headlines from linguistic parameters such as valence, arousal, and dominance. We then use simulation-based inference to learn a probabilistic mapping from brain maps to latent stimulus parameters. Our results show that these parameters can be recovered from predicted brain maps, validating the quality of neural encodings. They also show that LLMs can serve as controllable stimulus generators for simulated experiments. Together, these findings provide a step toward decoding and inverse design with foundation brain models.

</details>


### [103] [Gromov-Wasserstein Methods for Multi-View Relational Embedding and Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23912)
*Rafael Pereira Eufrazio,Eduardo Fernandes Montesuma,Charles Casimiro Cavalcante*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于Gromov-Wasserstein的Bary-GWMDS和Mean-GWMDS-C方法，直接从距离矩阵学习多视图数据的一致低维嵌入，有效处理跨视图非线性畸变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多视图关系数据中，不同视图的底层几何结构往往存在差异，现有方法难以在保留共享关系结构的同时处理非线性畸变。

Method: 提出Bary-GWMDS，基于Gromov-Wasserstein距离直接操作距离矩阵，学习保留共享关系结构的一致嵌入；进一步提出Mean-GWMDS-C，通过平均距离矩阵并利用一致Gromov-Wasserstein运输学习降维支持表示，适用于聚类任务。

Result: 在合成和真实数据集上，提出框架能够生成稳定且几何意义明确的嵌入。

Conclusion: 该方法有效处理多视图几何差异，获得几何意义明确的低维嵌入。

Abstract: Learning low-dimensional representations from multi-view relational data is challenging when underlying geometries differ across views. We propose Bary-GWMDS, a Gromov-Wasserstein-based method that operates directly on distance matrices to learn a consensus embedding preserving shared relational structure. By leveraging intrinsic distances, the approach naturally handles nonlinear distortions across views. We also introduce Mean-GWMDS-C, a clustering-oriented formulation that averages distance matrices and learns reduced-support representations via a consensus Gromov-Wasserstein transport. Experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets show that the proposed framework yields stable and geometrically meaningful embeddings.

</details>


### [104] [Reward Models Are Secretly Value Functions: Temporally Coherent Reward Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22981)
*Alex Nikulkov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出时序一致性奖励建模（TCRM），通过对标准Bradley-Terry损失附加两个正则化项（蒙特卡洛和时序差分价值学习目标），使得奖励模型在每一token位置的输出都能表征最终奖励的条件期望。该方法无需改变架构、数据或推理流程，即可实现：可解释的token级奖励轨迹（中间token成对准确率从50%提升至88.9%）、在ProcessBench上取得44.9%平均F1的SOTA结果、以及在PPO中统一奖励/价值建模（GPU峰值内存降低27%，步耗时减少19%，LLM质量不变）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的奖励模型仅对响应的最后一个token进行评分，丢弃了中间位置的丰富信号，导致token级别的输出存在噪声。本文认为这是一个被忽视的机会——理想情况下，奖励模型在任何token位置的输出应代表当前部分响应条件下最终奖励的条件期望。

Method: 在标准Bradley-Terry损失基础上，引入两个正则化项：蒙特卡洛（MC）和时序差分（TD）价值学习目标。理论证明其最小化器等价于条件期望。该方法无需改变架构、数据或推理过程。

Result: 中间token成对准确率从50%提升至88.9%，最终token准确率保持不变；在ProcessBench上取得44.9%平均F1，达到仅使用结果数据训练的模型中的SOTA；在PPO中实现统一奖励/价值建模，GPU峰值内存降低27%，步耗时减少19%，LLM生成质量不变。

Conclusion: TCRM通过一个统一原则（条件期望约束）同时解锁三种能力：可解释的token级奖励轨迹、SOTA的过程奖励建模、以及高效的统一奖励/价值建模，揭示了奖励模型中间层信号的重要价值。

Abstract: Reward models in RLHF are trained to score only the final token of a response - a choice that discards rich signal from every intermediate position and produces models whose token-level outputs are noise. We argue this is a missed opportunity: a well-trained reward model's output at any token should represent the conditional expectation of the final reward given the response so far. We introduce Temporally Coherent Reward Modeling (TCRM), which induces this property via two regularization terms on top of the standard Bradley-Terry loss, with minimizers provably equal to conditional expectations. The regularizers correspond to Monte Carlo and TD value-learning objectives, establishing a direct connection to RL value functions. TCRM requires zero changes to architecture, data, or inference, yet unlocks three capabilities from one principle: interpretable token-level reward trajectories (middle-token pairwise accuracy improved from 50% to 88.9%, final-token accuracy preserved); state-of-the-art PRM performance on ProcessBench (44.9% average F1) among models trained only on outcome data; and unified reward/value modeling in PPO, reducing peak GPU memory by 27% and step time by 19% with matching LLM quality.

</details>


### [105] [DecompKAN: Decomposed Patch-KAN for Long-Term Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23968)
*Naveen Mysore*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DecompKAN是一种轻量级、无注意力的时序预测架构，结合趋势-残差分解、通道级分片、学习实例归一化和B样条KAN边缘函数，在多个基准上达到最优或并列最优MSE，尤其擅长平滑动态和生理时间序列，并通过可视化学习边缘函数实现模型可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学领域（如气候建模、生理监测、能源系统）的准确时序预测既需要高预测性能，又需要模型透明度，而现有方法在这两者之间难以兼顾。

Method: 提出DecompKAN架构，包含：趋势-残差分解、通道级分片、学习实例归一化、B样条KAN边缘函数（每个KAN边缘学习显式的1D标量函数，可直接可视化）。

Result: 在标准基准上，32个数据集-预测长度组合中有15个达到最优或并列最优MSE；在9个数据集（含PPG-DaLiA生理基准）的36个受控同方案比较中有20个达到最优或并列最优。在Solar（-17%）、ECL（-10% vs iTransformer）、Weather等平滑动态数据集和生理时序上表现突出。

Conclusion: 架构管道（分解、分片、归一化）对性能的贡献大于非线性层的选择；KAN公式化使得学到的潜在变换可被检查和可视化，且不同领域展现出定性不同的潜在非线性特征。

Abstract: Accurate time series forecasting in scientific domains such as climate modeling, physiological monitoring, and energy systems benefits from both competitive predictions and model transparency. This work proposes DecompKAN, a lightweight attention-free architecture that combines trend-residual decomposition, channel-wise patching, learned instance normalization, and B-spline Kolmogorov-Arnold Network (KAN) edge functions. Each KAN edge learns an explicit, inspectable 1D scalar function over learned patch-embedding coordinates that can be directly visualized. On standard benchmarks, DecompKAN achieves best or tied-best MSE on 15 of 32 dataset-horizon combinations among selected published baselines, and achieves best or tied-best MSE on 20 of 36 comparisons under a controlled same-recipe evaluation across 9 datasets including the physiological PPG-DaLiA benchmark. The architecture shows particular strength on datasets with smooth temporal dynamics (Solar -17%, ECL -10% vs. iTransformer, Weather) and physiological time series. Visualization of learned edge functions reveals qualitatively different latent nonlinearities across domains. Ablation analysis shows that the architectural pipeline (decomposition, patching, normalization) drives performance more than the choice of nonlinear layer, while the KAN formulation enables inspection of learned latent transformations.

</details>


### [106] [Stochastic simultaneous optimistic optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24537)
*Michal Valko,Alexandra Carpentier,Rémi Munos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: StoSOO是一种无需知道函数局部平滑性先验信息的全局优化算法，通过乐观策略和层次分区构建置信上界，在有限噪声评估下实现接近最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于bandits的全局优化方法需要预先知道函数的局部平滑性（半度量），这在实际中难以获取。作者希望设计一种不依赖此类先验知识但仍能保证良好性能的算法。

Method: 提出StoSOO算法，采用乐观策略，在函数域的层次化分区上迭代构建置信上界，据此决定下一个采样点。算法仅假设函数在某个全局最大值附近相对于某半度量是局部光滑的，但无需知道该半度量的具体形式。

Result: 有限时间分析表明，即使不知道函数的局部光滑性，StoSOO的性能几乎与专门为该函数调优的最佳算法相当。

Conclusion: 在不依赖局部光滑性先验知识的前提下，通过乐观层次分区策略可以实现接近最优的全局优化性能，显著降低了对问题先验信息的要求。

Abstract: We study the problem of global maximization of a function f given a finite number of evaluations perturbed by noise. We consider a very weak assumption on the function, namely that it is locally smooth (in some precise sense) with respect to some semi-metric, around one of its global maxima. Compared to previous works on bandits in general spaces (Kleinberg et al., 2008; Bubeck et al., 2011a) our algorithm does not require the knowledge of this semi-metric. Our algorithm, StoSOO, follows an optimistic strategy to iteratively construct upper confidence bounds over the hierarchical partitions of the function domain to decide which point to sample next. A finite-time analysis of StoSOO shows that it performs almost as well as the best specifically-tuned algorithms even though the local smoothness of the function is not known.

</details>


### [107] [Efficient learning by implicit exploration in bandit problems with side observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24555)
*Tomas Kocak,Gergely Neu,Michal Valko,Remi Munos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对部分可观测的在线学习问题（介于全信息和bandit反馈之间），提出了两种基于隐式探索策略的算法，在未知观测系统的情况下实现了近乎最优的遗憾保证，并扩展到组合优化场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线学习算法在处理部分可观测反馈（如依赖动作的有向观测系统）时，通常需要事先知道观测结构才能选择动作，这限制了其在实际场景中的适用性。同时，组合优化问题中的反馈介于半bandit和全信息之间，缺乏高效的解决方案。

Method: 提出了隐式探索（implicit exploration）策略，该策略比以往的探索方法在计算和信息论上更高效。第一个算法适用于一般部分可观测模型，无需预先知道观测系统；第二个算法针对组合优化问题，在保证类似性质的同时实现计算高效，但调参机制稍复杂。

Result: 两个算法均达到近乎最优的遗憾界，且隐式探索策略在计算和信息效率上优于先前研究的探索策略。

Conclusion: 隐式探索策略为部分可观测在线学习问题提供了一种通用且高效的解决方案，填补了无需先验观测系统知识的算法空白，并为组合优化场景提供了计算可行的方案。

Abstract: We consider online learning problems under a partial observability model capturing situations where the information conveyed to the learner is between full information and bandit feedback. In the simplest variant, we assume that in addition to its own loss, the learner also gets to observe losses of some other actions. The revealed losses depend on the learner's action and a directed observation system chosen by the environment. For this setting, we propose the first algorithm that enjoys near-optimal regret guarantees without having to know the observation system before selecting its actions. Along similar lines, we also define a new partial information setting that models online combinatorial optimization problems where the feedback received by the learner is between semi-bandit and full feedback. As the predictions of our first algorithm cannot be always computed efficiently in this setting, we propose another algorithm with similar properties and with the benefit of always being computationally efficient, at the price of a slightly more complicated tuning mechanism. Both algorithms rely on a novel exploration strategy called implicit exploration, which is shown to be more efficient both computationally and information-theoretically than previously studied exploration strategies for the problem.

</details>


### [108] [Learning to Think from Multiple Thinkers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24737)
*Nirmit Joshi,Roey Magen,Nathan Srebro,Nikolaos Tsilivis,Gal Vardi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究多思维者（multiple thinkers）提供的链式思维（CoT）监督学习。在密码学假设下，证明了被动数据收集场景中，从两个或少数不同思维者的CoT监督学习可能是困难的。同时提出一种高效的主动学习算法，每个思维者只需少量CoT数据（与目标精度ε无关），思维者数量按log(1/ε)log log(1/ε)规模增长，并需要足够的被动端结果数据（规模约(1/ε)·polylog(1/ε)）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在多个不同思维者提供正确但系统性不同的CoT监督（如不同人写的数学题解题步骤、不同程序执行轨迹）下的学习复杂度，特别是在被动数据收集和主动学习两种设置下的理论界限。

Method: 理论分析：基于密码学假设证明被动多思维者CoT学习的困难性；设计通用且计算高效的主动学习算法，量化所需CoT数据量、思维者数量与被动端结果数据量的依赖关系。

Result: 1）在被动数据收集设置下，从两个或少数不同思维者的CoT监督学习是困难的（基于密码学假设）；2）主动学习算法中，每个思维者所需CoT数据量与ε无关，思维者数量按log(1/ε)log log(1/ε)增长，被动端结果数据需(1/ε)·polylog(1/ε)规模。

Conclusion: 多思维者CoT监督在被动设置下可能困难，但主动学习可以有效解决，给出了数据复杂度与精度之间的定量关系。

Abstract: We study learning with Chain-of-Thought (CoT) supervision from multiple thinkers, all of whom provide correct but possibly systematically different solutions, e.g., step-by-step solutions to math problems written by different thinkers, or step-by-step execution traces of different programs solving the same problem.
  We consider classes that are computationally easy to learn using CoT supervision from a single thinker, but hard to learn with only end-result supervision, i.e., without CoT (Joshi et al. 2025). We establish that, under cryptographic assumptions, learning can be hard from CoT supervision provided by two or a few different thinkers, in passive data-collection settings.
  On the other hand, we provide a generic computationally efficient active learning algorithm that learns with a small amount of CoT data per thinker that is completely independent of the target accuracy $\varepsilon$, a moderate number of thinkers that scales as $\log \frac{1}{\varepsilon}\log \log \frac{1}{\varepsilon}$, and sufficient passive end-result data that scales as $\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\cdot poly\log\frac{1}{\varepsilon}$.

</details>


### [109] [Preserving Long-Tailed Expert Information in Mixture-of-Experts Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23036)
*Haoze He,Xingyuan Ding,Xuan Jiang,Xinkai Zou,Alex Cheng,Yibo Zhao,Juncheng Billy Li,Heather Miller*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对MoE模型在SFT过程中因路由器脆弱导致的专家坍缩问题，提出一种无辅助损失的微调框架，通过偏置驱动的稀疏化与始终激活的冷凝专家结合，在无需辅助平衡损失的情况下提升长尾专家知识保留和模型性能，在数学推理和常识问答任务上平均提升2.5%以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE模型在SFT时路由器层脆弱，现有方法（如DenseMixer、ESFT）通过密集混合或辅助负载均衡损失来缓解专家坍缩，但这些方法引入的噪声梯度往往导致性能下降。初步实验发现即使很少被激活的专家也编码了对下游任务有用的非平凡知识，因此需要一种既能保留长尾专家知识、又避免辅助损失噪声的SFT方法。

Method: 提出无辅助损失的MoE SFT框架，包含两个核心设计：(1) 偏置驱动的稀疏化——鼓励任务相关专家保持活跃，推动长尾专家趋于不活跃，而非强制所有专家均衡激活；(2) 始终激活的冷凝专家（gated condenser experts）——提供持久的可学习路径，缓解梯度饥饿，促进原本因稀疏激活而碎片化的信息整合。

Result: 在大规模MoE模型上的实验表明，所提方法在数学推理和常识QA基准上优于DenseMixer和ESFT等基线，平均提升超过2.5%。

Conclusion: 所提设计在稀疏路由下能更好地保留长尾专家信息，证明了无需辅助损失即可有效进行MoE SFT的可行性。

Abstract: Despite MoE models leading many benchmarks, supervised fine-tuning (SFT) for the MoE architectures remains difficult because its router layers are fragile. Methods such as DenseMixer and ESFT mitigate router collapse with dense mixing or auxiliary load-balancing losses, but these introduce noisy gradients that often degrade performance. In preliminary experiments, we systematically pruned experts and observed that while certain super experts are activated far more frequently, discarding less used experts still leads to notable performance degradation. This suggests that even rarely activated experts encode non-trivial knowledge useful for downstream tasks. Motivated by this, we propose an auxiliary-loss-free MoE SFT framework that combines bias-driven sparsification with always-active gated condenser experts. Rather than enforcing balanced activation across all experts, our method encourages task-relevant experts to remain active while pushing long-tailed experts toward inactivity. The condenser experts provide a persistent, learnable pathway that alleviates gradient starvation and facilitates consolidation of information that would otherwise remain fragmented across sparsely activated experts. Analysis further suggest that this design better preserves long-tailed expert information under sparse routing. Experiments on large-scale MoE models demonstrate that our approach outperforms state-of-the-art SFT baselines such as DenseMixer and ESFT, achieving average gain of 2.5%+ on both mathematical reasoning and commonsenseQA benchmarks.

</details>


### [110] [The Optimal Sample Complexity of Multiclass and List Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24749)
*Chirag Pabbaraju*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了多类假设类的最大超图密度不超过其DS维数，解决了Daniely和Shalev-Shwartz（2014）的长期猜想，从而确定了多类分类和列表学习在样本复杂度上关于DS维数的最优依赖关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二元分类在VC维下的最优样本复杂度已明确，但多类分类的最优样本复杂度一直未解决。尽管DS维数被认为是多类分类的合适复杂度参数，但上下界之间始终存在√DS的差距。

Method: 基于Hanneke等人（2026）关于多类假设类DS维数的代数刻画，证明多类假设类的最大超图密度上界为DS维数。

Result: 证明了Daniely和Shalev-Shwartz（2014）的猜想，即最大超图密度≤DS维数，从而闭合了上下界之间的√DS差距。

Conclusion: 确定了多类分类以及列表学习在样本复杂度上关于DS维数的最优依赖关系。

Abstract: While the optimal sample complexity of binary classification in terms of the VC dimension is well-established, determining the optimal sample complexity of multiclass classification has remained open. The appropriate complexity parameter for multiclass classification is the DS dimension, and despite significant efforts, a gap of $\sqrt{\text{DS}}$ has persisted between the upper and lower bounds on sample complexity.
  Recent work by Hanneke et al. (2026) shows a novel algebraic characterization of multiclass hypothesis classes in terms of their DS dimension. Building up on this, we show that the maximum hypergraph density of any multiclass hypothesis class is upper-bounded by its DS dimension. This proves a longstanding conjecture of Daniely and Shalev-Shwartz (2014). As a consequence, we determine the optimal dependence of the sample complexity on the DS dimension for multiclass as well as list learning.

</details>


### [111] [A Differentiable Framework for Global Circulation Model Precipitation Bias Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23045)
*Kamlesh Sawadekar,Seth McGinnis,Peijun Li,Chaopeng Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为δCLIMBA（或dCLIMBA）的可微分偏差调整框架，通过学习历史CMIP6模型输出与参考再分析数据集之间的时空自适应参数化偏差调整过程，有效校正了GCM降水输出的系统性偏差，在极端事件捕捉、分位数分布重现和空间模式保持方面表现优异，且具备未来趋势保持和跨区域泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统统计方法无法从大数据中学习且难以解决GCM的系统性偏差，而机器学习方法虽具灵活性但其黑箱特性阻碍了对偏差的完整理解，限制了其在不同GCM和地理位置的泛化能力，尤其在降水偏差校正方面。

Method: 提出δCLIMBA框架，一种可微分的偏差调整方法，学习历史CMIP6模型输出与Livneh参考再分析数据集之间的时空自适应参数化偏差调整过程。

Result: 准确校正极端风暴事件的量级和分布，在捕捉极值方面表现尤为突出；在美国多个城市较好地重现了降水分位数分布；空间模式与广泛使用的LOCA2统计降尺度技术表现相当；保持了未来趋势；在未见区域上的偏差校正结果显示边际偏差得到减弱。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种模块化、计算高效且可扩展的偏差校正方法，具有物理信息、可扩展性，兼容历史和未来应用，适合集成到地球系统后处理流程和影响评估工作流中。

Abstract: Systematic biases in Global Circulation Model (GCM) outputs limit their direct applicability in regional planning, necessitating bias correction. Correcting precipitation is particularly challenging due to its non-Gaussian distribution, intermittent nature, and non-linear extremes. However, traditional statistical methods cannot learn from big data and easily address systematic biases in the GCMs, and while machine learning does provide this flexibility, their black-box type functionality hinders us from understanding these biases completely which also further prevents generalization across different GCMs and locations, especially for precipitation. In this study, we propose a differentiable bias adjustment framework called δCLIMBA (or dCLIMBA), that learns a spatiotemporally adaptive parametric bias adjustment procedure between historical CMIP6 model outputs and reference reanalysis datasets (Livneh). Results demonstrate that the proposed method accurately corrects both the magnitude and distribution of extreme storm events, with particularly strong performance in capturing extremes. The quantile distribution of precipitation is well reproduced across diverse U.S. cities, and spatial patterns perform comparably to the widely used LOCA2 statistical downscaling technique. In addition, the framework showed future trend preservation unlike pure quantile based methods and LOCA2; and results from bias correction over unseen regions showed that the marginal biases were attenuated. This work presents a modular, computationally efficient and extensible bias correction approach that is physically informed, scalable, and compatible with both historical and future applications. Its flexibility makes it suitable for integration into Earth system post-processing pipelines and impact workflows.

</details>


### [112] [C-MORAL: Controllable Multi-Objective Molecular Optimization with Reinforcement Alignment for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23061)
*Rui Gao,Youngseung Jeon,Swastik Roy,Morteza Ziyadi,Xiang 'Anthony' Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: C-Moral提出了一种基于强化学习后训练的框架，通过分组相对优化、属性分数对齐和非线性奖励聚合，实现了可控的多目标分子优化，在C-MuMOInstruct基准上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在分子优化方面有潜力，但如何使其对齐选择性且相互竞争的药物设计约束仍然是一个挑战。

Method: 提出了C-Moral框架，包含三部分：基于分组的相对优化、异质目标的属性分数对齐、以及连续非线性奖励聚合，以提高跨竞争属性的稳定性。

Result: 在C-MuMOInstruct基准上，C-Moral在域内任务上达到48.9%的SOR，域外任务上达到39.5%，均优于现有模型，同时较好地保留了骨架相似性。

Conclusion: RL后训练是使分子语言模型对齐连续分子设计目标的有效方式。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) show promise for molecular optimization, but aligning them with selective and competing drug-design constraints remains challenging. We propose C-Moral, a reinforcement learning post-training framework for controllable multi-objective molecular optimization. C-Moral combines group-based relative optimization, property score alignment for heterogeneous objectives, and continuous non-linear reward aggregation to improve stability across competing properties. Experiments on the C-MuMOInstruct benchmark show that C-Moral consistently outperforms state-of-the-art models across both in-domain and out-of-domain settings, achieving the best Success Optimized Rate (SOR) of 48.9% on IND tasks and 39.5% on OOD tasks, while largely preserving scaffold similarity. These results suggest that RL post-training is an effective way to align molecular language models with continuous molecular design objectives. Our code and models are publicly available at https://github.com/Rwigie/C-MORAL.

</details>


### [113] [Channel Adaptation for EEG Foundation Models: A Systematic Benchmark Across Architectures, Tasks, and Training Regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23091)
*Kuntal Kokate,Bruno Aristimunha,Dung Truong,Arnaud Delorme*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统比较了四种通道适配方法（Conv1d投影、球面样条插值、源空间分解、黎曼重中心化）在五个预训练EEG基础模型、五个下游任务和两种训练机制下的表现。研究发现：刚性脑电模型需要外部适配，柔性模型在微调时原生表现更好但在冻结编码器时需外部方法；存在探针-SFT不对称性；最优方法依赖架构；仅5M参数的CBraMod在4/5数据集上优于31倍更大的模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展EEG基础模型需要聚合来自异构电极蒙太奇的数据，这是构建更大预训练语料库和下游部署的前提条件，但目前缺乏对不同通道适配方法的系统性比较。

Method: 系统比较四种通道适配方法（Conv1d投影、球面样条插值、源空间分解、黎曼重中心化），在五种预训练EEG基础模型（5M-157M参数）、五个下游任务和两种训练机制（微调和冻结编码器）下进行评估，每种设置使用10-15个随机种子。

Result: 1) 刚性脑电模型（BENDR, Neuro-GPT）需要外部适配；2) 柔性模型（EEGPT, CBraMod）在微调时原生表现匹配或超越外部方法，但在冻结编码器下需要外部方法；3) 存在探针-SFT不对称性，外部适配可能在柔性模型微调时造成严重负迁移；4) 最优方法依赖架构（Conv1d适合BENDR，SSI/Riemannian适合Neuro-GPT，源空间分解适合抑郁检测）；5) 5M参数的CBraMod在4/5数据集上优于31倍更大的模型。

Conclusion: 通道适配方法的选择需综合考虑模型架构和训练范式，紧凑型EEG专用架构可媲美更大规模模型，为EEG基础模型的规模化提供了实践指导。

Abstract: Scaling EEG foundation models requires pooling data across heterogeneous electrode montages, a prerequisite both for larger pretraining corpora and for downstream deployment. We present the first systematic comparison of four channel adaptation methods (Conv1d projection, spherical spline interpolation (SSI), source-space decomposition, and Riemannian re-centering) across five pretrained EEG foundation models (5M--157M parameters), five downstream tasks, and two training regimes with 10--15 random seeds each. We find that rigid-montage models (BENDR, Neuro-GPT) require external adaptation, while flexible models (EEGPT, CBraMod) match or exceed it natively when fine-tuned but benefit from external methods under frozen-encoder deployment. A probe-SFT asymmetry exists: external adaptation can cause severe negative transfer during fine-tuning of flexible models. The optimal method is architecture-dependent (Conv1d for BENDR, SSI/Riemannian for Neuro-GPT, source-space decomposition for depression detection), and 5M-parameter CBraMod outperforms models up to 31$\times$ larger on 4/5 datasets, consistent with independent findings that compact EEG-specific architectures can match larger models.

</details>


### [114] [Unstable Rankings in Bayesian Deep Learning Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23102)
*Qishi Zhan,Minxuan Hu,Guansu Wang,Jiaxin Liu,Liang He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示在数据稀缺条件下，贝叶斯深度学习方法的性能评估不可靠，方法排名存在数据集依赖性和样本量依赖性。作者提出贝叶斯层次模型将评估指标视为随机变量，并引入预测最小可检测差异曲线，为低数据场景下的方法比较提供了不确定性感知的评估框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准贝叶斯深度学习评估假设指标估计可靠，但该假设在数据稀缺时失败。方法排名在小样本下不可靠且存在数据集依赖性，点估计无法揭示这种不确定性，且不存在通用的样本量阈值，因此需要数据集特定的后验推断。

Method: 构建贝叶斯层次模型，引入方法特定方差，将评估指标视为跨数据实现的随机变量；使用预测最小可检测差异曲线判断给定训练规模下观测差异是否可检测。

Result: 在6种贝叶斯深度学习方法、5个回归数据集上，低数据场景下方法优越性的证据与预测可检测性之间存在显著分歧，不确定性感知评估十分必要。

Conclusion: 该框架为实践者提供了判断评估数据是否充分的原理性工具，有助于在得出方法优劣结论前进行可靠的评估。

Abstract: Standard evaluations of Bayesian deep learning methods assume that metric estimates are reliable, but we show this assumption fails under data scarcity. Method rankings are not only unreliable at small $n$, but also dataset-dependent in ways that point estimates cannot reveal: the same method comparison yields $P(\mathrm{MCD} \prec \mathrm{Ensemble}) = 1.000$ at $n = 50$ on one dataset and remains below $0.95$ even at $n = 500$ on another. Across the datasets we consider, no universal sample size threshold exists, which is precisely why dataset-specific posterior inference is necessary. To address this, we use a Bayesian hierarchical model with method-specific variances to treat evaluation metrics as random variables across data realizations, and we use a predictive Minimum Detectable Difference curve to assess whether an observed gap would be detectable at a given training size. Across six Bayesian deep learning methods and five regression datasets, our results show that uncertainty-aware evaluation is necessary in low-data settings, because current evidence for method superiority and predictive detectability at the same training size can diverge substantially. Our framework provides practitioners with principled tools to determine whether their evaluation data is sufficient before drawing conclusions about method superiority.

</details>


### [115] [Conditional Imputation for Within-Modality Missingness in Multi-Modal Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23112)
*Wugeng Zheng,Ziwen Kan,Katie Wang,Chen Chen,Song Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CondI是一个面向多模态联邦学习中模态缺失问题的条件扩散模型框架，通过显式数据插补恢复缺失的时序模态，在三个临床数据集上达到与SOTA可比的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态联邦学习在临床应用中常因传感器中断或采样不规则导致模态内缺失，现有方法通过架构对齐或缺失嵌入隐式处理未观测数据，无法恢复真实数据分布，性能欠佳。CondI旨在显式解决这一缺失问题。

Method: 采用两阶段训练流程：第一阶段利用可用多模态上下文和条件嵌入，通过条件扩散模型插补缺失的时序组件；第二阶段优化模态特定提取器和联合嵌入空间。推理时，插补后的原始数据通过训练好的提取器生成鲁棒特征。

Result: 在PTB-XL、SLEEP-EDF、MIMIC-IV三个临床数据集上，CondI达到了与现有最先进基线可比的结果。

Conclusion: 显式数据插补确保模型在完整的语义结构上运行，显著增强了对严重数据不完整性的鲁棒性，为多模态联邦学习中模态缺失问题提供了一种有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Multimodal Federated Learning (MMFL) enables privacy-preserving collaborative training, but real-world clinical applications often suffer from within-modality missingness caused by sensor intermittency or irregular sampling. Existing methods implicitly represent unobserved data via architectural alignment or missing embeddings, often failing to recover the true distribution and yielding sub-optimal performance. We propose CondI, a federated framework explicitly addressing this missingness using conditional diffusion models.
